Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The paleobiology operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 590. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 835 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 590 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the paleobiology structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 835. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
